  "MOBILE" VARCHAR2(32),

  "NATION" VARCHAR2(32),

  "CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256)
);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_METHOD(
    ID VARCHAR2(32) NOT NULL,
    METHODNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_METHOD IS '方法';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.METHODNAME IS '方法名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_METHOD  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_METHOD PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    METHODID NUMBER NULL,
    VERSIONNO NUMBER NULL,
    TESTMETHODBASIS VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    BEGINDATE DATE NULL,
    EXPIREDATE DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION IS '方法版本';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.METHODID IS '方法编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.VERSIONNO IS '版本号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.TESTMETHODBASIS IS '检测方法及依据';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.BEGINDATE IS '开始日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.EXPIREDATE IS '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_METHODVERSION PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_METHODVERSION_METHODID ON T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION (METHODID);

COMMIT;

-- Create table
create table T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY
(
  id               NUMBER not null,
  mtlcategoryid    VARCHAR2(16),
  mtlcategoryname  VARCHAR2(64),
  mtlcategorydesc  VARCHAR2(256),
  createdbyid      VARCHAR2(32),
  createdbyname    VARCHAR2(64),
  createdtime      DATE,
  createdbyorgid   VARCHAR2(32),
  createdbyorgname VARCHAR2(256)
);
-- Add comments to the table
comment on table T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY
  is '材料类型表';
-- Add comments to the columns
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.id
  is '主键';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.mtlcategoryid
  is '材料类型编码';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.mtlcategoryname
  is '材料类型名称';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.mtlcategorydesc
  is '描述';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.createdbyid
  is '制单人编码';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.createdbyname
  is '制单人名称';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.createdtime
  is '制单时间';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.createdbyorgid
  is '制单人单位编码';
comment on column T_EMC_MTL_CATEGORY.createdbyorgname
  is '制单人单位名称';

  "NAME" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "TELEPHONE" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "MAILBOX" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "FAX" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "POSTALCODE" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "ADDRESS" NVARCHAR2(512),
  "JOB" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "PROVINCE" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "CITY" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "AREA" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "REMARK" NVARCHAR2(512),
  "CREATEDBYID" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYNAME" NVARCHAR2(64),
  "CREATEDTIME" DATE,
  "CREATEDBYORGID" NVARCHAR2(32),
  "CREATEDBYORGNAME" NVARCHAR2(256)
)
;

  IS '监测模板ID';

  IS '监测方案名称';

  IS '分析项组编码';

  IS '分析项组';

  IS '制单人名称';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CERT(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    EMPID VARCHAR2(32)  NOT NULL,
    CERTNO VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CERTNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    AUTHDATE DATE NULL,
    EXPIREDATE DATE NULL,
    WARNINGDAYS NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    STATUS NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    ORDERNO NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CERT IS '人员证书表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.ID IS 'ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.EMPID IS '人员编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CERTNO IS '证书编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CERTNAME IS '证书名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.AUTHDATE IS '授权日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.EXPIREDATE IS '过期日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.WARNINGDAYS IS '提前提醒（天）';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.STATUS IS '状态';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.orderNo IS '排序码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_CERT  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_CERT PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    CERTID NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    METHODID NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(512)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD IS '方法内容表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD.ID IS '联系人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD.CERTID IS '证书ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD.METHODID IS '方法编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD.REMARK IS '备注';

 );

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_CERTLOG  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_CERTLOG PRIMARY KEY (ID);

DROP TABLE T_EMC_CERT;
DROP TABLE T_EMC_CERTLOG;
DROP TABLE T_EMC_CERTMONITORABILITY;
DROP TABLE T_EMC_CERTTESTMETHOD;

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CERT(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    EMPID VARCHAR2(32)  NOT NULL,
    CERTNO VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CERTNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    AUTHDATE DATE NULL,
    EXPIREDATE DATE NULL,
    WARNINGDAYS NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    STATUS VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    ORDERNO NUMBER(20)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CERT IS '人员证书表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.ID IS 'ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.EMPID IS '人员编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CERTNO IS '证书编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CERTNAME IS '证书名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.AUTHDATE IS '授权日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.EXPIREDATE IS '过期日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.WARNINGDAYS IS '提前提醒（天）';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.STATUS IS '状态';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.ORDERNO IS '排序码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CERT.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    MONITORNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    MONITOROBJID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    MONITOROBJNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    PROJCATEGORY VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    METHODSOURCE VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    AUTHENTICATION VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    APPROVAL VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    INSTRUCTION VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(512)  NULL,
    STATUS VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_RUN(
 ID VARCHAR2(15) NOT NULL,
     HUMIDITY VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
     TEMPERATURE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
     ANALYSISBEGINDATE DATE  NULL,
     ANALYSISBEGINTIME VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
     ANALYSISENDDATE DATE  NULL,
     ANALYSISENDTIME VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
     RECEIVEDATE DATE  NULL,
     RECEIVETIME VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
     HANDLEDATE DATE   NULL,
     HANDLEMETHOD VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
     BEFOREHANDLE VARCHAR2(66) NULL,
     METHODPARAM VARCHAR2(66) NULL,
     ANALYSISUSERID VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
     ANALYSISUSERNAME VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
     PARTAKEUSERID VARCHAR2(240) NULL,
     PARTAKEUSERNAME VARCHAR2(480)  NULL,
     ASSISTEQUIPTID NUMBER   NULL,
     EQUIPTID NUMBER NULL,
     CALIBRATIONCURVE VARCHAR2(240) NULL,
     ORDERCATEGORY VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
     CHECKUSERID VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
     CHECKUSERNAME VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
     TESTMETHODID NUMBER NULL,
     TESTNAME VARCHAR2(66) NULL,
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(33) NULL,
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(66) NULL,
     CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(33) NULL,
     CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(255) NULL
 );

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_RUN IS '样品测试';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.id IS '批号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.HUMIDITY IS '湿度';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.TEMPERATURE IS '温度';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISBEGINDATE IS '分析开始日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISBEGINTIME IS '分析开始时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISENDDATE IS '分析开始日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISENDTIME IS '分析开始时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.RECEIVEDATE IS '样品接受日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.RECEIVETIME IS '样品接受时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.HANDLEDATE IS '处置日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.HANDLEMETHOD IS '处置方式';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.BEFOREHANDLE IS '前处理';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.METHODPARAM IS '方法参数';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISUSERID IS '分析员编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ANALYSISUSERNAME IS '分析员';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.PARTAKEUSERID IS '共同完成人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.PARTAKEUSERNAME IS '共同完成人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ASSISTEQUIPTID IS '辅助仪器';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.EQUIPTID IS '现场仪器';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.CALIBRATIONCURVE IS '校准曲线';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.ORDERCATEGORY IS '样品类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.CHECKUSERID IS '校核人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.CHECKUSERNAME IS '校核人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.TESTMETHODID IS '测试方法编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.TESTNAME IS '测试名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RUN.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

drop table T_EMC_ORDER;


CREATE TABLE T_EMC_ORDER(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ORDERNO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    PROJID NUMBER NULL,
    FOLDERID NUMBER NULL,
    LABELNO VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CLIENTORDERNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PARENTORDERNO VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    ORDERDESC VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    SENDDATE DATE NULL,
    SENDTIME VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CYCLE NUMBER NULL,
    FREQUENCY NUMBER NULL,
    SAMPLEID VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    SAMPLEBEGINDATE DATE NULL,
    SAMPLEBEGINTIME VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    SAMPLECOMPLETEDATE DATE NULL,
    SAMPLECOMPLETETIME VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    SAMPLENUM NUMBER NULL,
    QCCATEGORYID NUMBER NULL,
    QCCATEGORYCODE VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    QCNAME VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    MONITORTMPLID VARCHAR2(18)  NOT NULL,
    ORDERCATEGORY VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    ORDERNUM NUMBER NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_ORDER IS '样品';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.orderNo IS '样品编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.projId IS '项目编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.folderId IS '点位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.labelNo IS '标签编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.clientOrderName IS '客户样品名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.parentOrderNo IS '父样编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.orderDesc IS '样品描述';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sendDate IS '送样日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sendTime IS '送样时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.cycle IS '周期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.frequency IS '频次';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleId IS '采样单编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleBeginDate IS '采样开始日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleBeginTime IS '采样开始时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleCompleteDate IS '采样完成日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleCompleteTime IS '采样完成时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.sampleNum IS '采样数';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.qcCategoryId IS 'QC类型主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.qcCategoryCode IS 'QC类型编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.qcName IS 'QC类型中文';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.remark IS '备注';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER.createdById IS '制单人编码';

drop table T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT;


CREATE TABLE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    FOLDERID NUMBER NULL,
    ORDERID NUMBER NULL,
    PROJID NUMBER NULL,
    ORDERTASKID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTMETHODID NUMBER NULL,
    METHODID NUMBER NULL,
    METHODVERSIONID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMORDERNO NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    ITEMALIASNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    REVISIONRULE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    UNITNAME VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REPEATNO NUMBER NULL,
    REPEATQTY NUMBER NULL,
    ORIGINALRESULT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    REPORTRESULT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    REPORTDISPLAY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    RESULTSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    ANALYSISTYPE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    LOWLIMIT NUMBER NULL,
    HIGHLIMIT NUMBER NULL,
    INDEXLOWLIMIT NUMBER NULL,
    INDEXHIGHLIMIT NUMBER NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT IS '样品结果';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT.folderId IS '点位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT.orderId IS '样品编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT.projId IS '项目编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT.orderTaskId IS '样品测试编码';

values (SEQ_CORE.NEXTVAL, '现场质控', 'qualityUseType', null, 1, 'admin', '系统管理员', null, '0', null, null, null, 'scene');

commit;

    ADDRESS VARCHAR2(513)  NULL,
    PHONE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,

    MAIL VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    SCALE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,

    LONGITUDE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    LATITUDE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,

-- 留样管理
-- 创建留样表 T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN
CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN" (
	"ID" NUMBER(20,0) NOT NULL,
	"ORDERID" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"PROJID" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"FOLDERID" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"RETAINQTY" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"RETAINTIMELIMIT" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"RETAINLOC" VARCHAR2(66) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"RETAINUSERID" VARCHAR2(30) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"RETAINDAYS" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"HANDLEUSERID" VARCHAR2(30) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"HANDLEDATE" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"HANDLERECORD" VARCHAR2(120) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(32) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDTIME" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(32) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	PRIMARY KEY ("ID")
)
NOCOMPRESS
NOPARALLEL ;
COMMENT ON TABLE "T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN" IS '留样表';

	"CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDTIME" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(32) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	"CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(256) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
	PRIMARY KEY ("ID")
)
NOCOMPRESS
NOPARALLEL ;

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FILE"."CREATEDBYID" IS '创建人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FILE"."CREATEDBYNAME" IS '创建人姓名';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    COURSECODE VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    COURSENAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TRAINCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TRAINCONTENTCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TRAINHOUR NUMBER NULL,
    LECTURER VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    FEE NUMBER(10,2) NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(258)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE IS '培训课程';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.courseCode IS '课程编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.courseName IS '课程名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.trainCategory IS '培训类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.trainContentCategory IS '培训内容类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.trainHour IS '培训课时';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.lecturer IS '主讲人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.fee IS '费用';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.remark IS '摘要';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-03 add by yuanhh--------------begin
UPDATE T_EMC_METHOD T
   SET T.MAXVERSIONID =
       (SELECT MAX(ID) FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE METHODID = T.ID GROUP BY METHODID),
       T.MAXVERSIONNO =
       (SELECT MAX(VERSIONNO) FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE METHODID = T.ID GROUP BY METHODID);

UPDATE T_EMC_METHOD T
   SET T.TESTMETHODBASIS =
       (SELECT TESTMETHODBASIS FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE ID=T.MAXVERSIONID),
       T.MAXELNTMPLID =
       (SELECT ELNTMPLID FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE ID=T.MAXVERSIONID);
COMMIT;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-12 add by zhanghang--------------begin
CREATE OR REPLACE
PROCEDURE P_GETELNDSSQL_ORDERTASK(IN_RUNID IN VARCHAR2,OUT_ELNSQL OUT VARCHAR2)
AS
		CURSOR CUR_ITEMALIASNAMES IS
							SELECT DISTINCT OTR.ITEMALIASNAME
							FROM T_EMC_ORDER_TASK OT, T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT OTR
							WHERE OT.ID = OTR.ORDERTASKID AND OT.RUNID = IN_RUNID AND OTR.REPEATNO = 1;
    ITEMALIASNAME VARCHAR2(64);
BEGIN
		OUT_ELNSQL := 'SELECT O.ORDERNO ''样品编号'', ';
    OPEN CUR_ITEMALIASNAMES;
    LOOP
        FETCH CUR_ITEMALIASNAMES INTO ITEMALIASNAME;
        --循环退出条件一定要写
        EXIT WHEN CUR_ITEMALIASNAMES%NOTFOUND;
        --显示出每次用游标取出的值
				OUT_ELNSQL := OUT_ELNSQL || 'MAX(CASE OTR.ITEMALIASNAME WHEN ''' ||
													ITEMALIASNAME ||
													''' THEN OTR.REPORTRESULT ELSE NULL END ) ' ||
													'''' ||
													ITEMALIASNAME ||''',';

				OUT_ELNSQL := OUT_ELNSQL ||
													'MAX(CASE OTR.ITEMALIASNAME WHEN ''' ||
													ITEMALIASNAME ||
													''' THEN OTR.ID ELSE NULL END ) ' ||
													'''' ||
													ITEMALIASNAME ||
													'_RID'',';
    END LOOP;
		CLOSE CUR_ITEMALIASNAMES;

	 OUT_ELNSQL := SUBSTR(OUT_ELNSQL,1,LENGTH(OUT_ELNSQL)-1);
	 OUT_ELNSQL := OUT_ELNSQL ||
			' FROM T_EMC_ORDER_TASK OT,T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT OTR,T_EMC_ORDER O WHERE OT.ID = OTR.ORDERTASKID AND OT.ORDERID = O.ID AND OT.RUNID = ''' ||
			IN_RUNID ||
			''' AND OTR.REPEATNO =1 GROUP BY O.ORDERNO';

		IF(INSTR(OUT_ELNSQL,',  FROM T_EMC_ORDER_TASK OT')>0)
			THEN
				OUT_ELNSQL := NULL;
		END IF;
END P_GETELNDSSQL_ORDERTASK;
-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-12 add by zhanghang--------------end

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL(
    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    MTLNAME VARCHAR2(72)  NULL,
    SPECIFICATION VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    PRICE NUMBER(15,4)  NULL,
    QUANTITY NUMBER(15,2)  NULL,
    TOTAL NUMBER(15,4)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    MODEL VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    LVL VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    VENDOR VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    PURCHASEDATE DATE  NULL,
    USEDORGNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CHECKMETHOD VARCHAR2(72)  NULL,
    CHECKRECORD VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CHECKRESULT VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    HANDLEOPINION VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL IS '常规物资';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL.ID IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL.TOTAL IS '合计';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL.REMARK IS '备注';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL.MODEL IS '型号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL.LVL IS '等级';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF(
    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    ACQSCRIPTID NUMBER  NULL,
    INTERFACECATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    BAUDRATE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TERMINATOR VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    DATABIT VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    PARITYCHECK VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    COM VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    STOPBIT VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    BEGINSYMBOL VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    ENDSYMBOL VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    ANALYTICFORMULA VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    IP VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF IS '设备-DCU配置';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.acqScriptId IS '采集脚本ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.interfaceCategory IS '接口类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.baudRate IS '波特率';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.terminator IS '终止字符串';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.dataBit IS '数据位';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.parityCheck IS '奇偶校验';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.com IS 'COM口';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.stopBit IS '停止位';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.beginSymbol IS '开始标记';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.endSymbol IS '结束标记';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.analyticFormula IS '数据解析公式';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.ip IS 'ip地址';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE(
    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    EQUIPTID NUMBER  NULL,
    SOURCEFILENAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    TARGETFILENAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    CONTENT CLOB  NULL,
    RESULT CLOB  NULL,
    STATUS NUMBER  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE IS '仪器采集文件';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.equiptId IS '仪器ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.sourceFileName IS '源文件名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.targetFileName IS '目录文件名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.content IS '文件文本内容';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.result IS '解析文件的字符串结果';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.status IS '处理状态0未处理，1已处理';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE.createdTime IS '制单时间';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE PRIMARY KEY (ID);

    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,

    FILENAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY(
    ID VARCHAR2(18)  NOT NULL,
    APPLICANT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TYPE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    ORGDUTY VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    STARTDATE DATE NULL,
    ENDDATE DATE NULL,
    DAYS NUMBER NULL,
    NEEDCREDENTIALS VARCHAR2(3)  NULL,
    CREDENTIALS VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    VISITPLACE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    STAYDAYS NUMBER NULL,
    REVERTDATE DATE NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(240)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY IS '日常请假表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.id IS '申请编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.applicant IS '请假人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.type IS '请假类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.orgDuty IS '所在单位及职务';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.startDate IS '请假开始时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.endDate IS '请假结束时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.days IS '请假天数';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_LEAVE_APPLY.needCredentials IS '是否取证';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CURVE(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    CURVENO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    FOLDERID NUMBER NULL,
    EQUIPTID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    METHODID NUMBER NULL,
    METHODNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    ITEMID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    INTERCEPT VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    SLOPE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    COEFFICIENT VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    CONFDATE DATE NULL,
    EXPIREDATE DATE NULL,
    MARKINGNO VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    MARKINGNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    MARKINGCONCENT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    MARKINGUNITNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    MARKINGVALIDATE NUMBER NULL,
    MARKINGMANUFACTURER VARCHAR2(240)  NULL,
    USECONCENT VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    USECONCENTUNITNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    COLORVOLUME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    SOLCONF VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CURVECONDITION VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    STATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CURVE IS '曲线表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.methodId IS '方法编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.itemId IS '分析项编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.itemName IS '分析项名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.intercept IS '截距';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.slope IS '斜率';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.coefficient IS '相关系数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.confDate IS '配置日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.expireDate IS '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.markingNo IS '标液编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.markingName IS '标液名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.markingConcent IS '标液浓度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.markingUnitName IS '标液单位';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CURVE.colorVolume IS '显色体积';

    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    PATROLDATE DATE  NOT NULL,
    PATROLWEEKDAY VARCHAR2(9)  NOT NULL,
    PATROLTIME DATE  NULL,
    PATROLUSERID NUMBER(18)  NULL,
    PROBLEM VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    HANDLEUSERIDS VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    HANDLEUSERNAMES VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    HANDLEREBACK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    HANDLERESULT VARCHAR2(1)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(32)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(256)  NULL
 );

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PATROL.handleUserNames IS '处理人员名字';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PATROL.handleReback IS '处理反馈';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PATROL.handleResult IS '状态';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PATROL.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PATROL.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TMPLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    USEDORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL IS '上报模板';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.id IS '模板编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.tmplName IS '模板名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.usedOrgId IS '应用部门';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL.processStatus IS '流程状态';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_INFO_TMPL PRIMARY KEY (ID);

    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    INFOTMPLID NUMBER NULL,
    FIELD VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FIELDTITLE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,

    UNITNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,

    TRANSFORM VARCHAR2(3)  NULL,
    PRIMARYINDEX NUMBER NULL,
    PRIMARYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    BIZFIELD VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    DEFAULTVALUE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD IS '上报模板字段';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.infoTmplId IS '上报模板ID';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.unitName IS '单位名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.revisionRule IS '修约规则';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.transform IS '是否转换列';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.primaryIndex IS '主键列数';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.primaryName IS '主键名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.bizField IS '对应系统字段';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD.createdById IS '制单人编码';

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-21 add by zhangsy--------------begin
INSERT INTO T_EMC_PROPERTY_DATA ( id, bizid, tmplid ) SELECT
SEQ_CORE.NEXTVAL,
A.ID,
B.MONITORPOINTTYPE
FROM
    T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT A
    LEFT JOIN T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT_CATEGORY B ON A.MONITORPOINTCATEGORYID = B.ID
    WHERE
    NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT TMPLID, BIZID FROM T_EMC_PROPERTY_DATA C WHERE C.BIZID = A.ID
    )
commit;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_RESULT_LOG(
    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    ORDERTASKID NUMBER  NULL,
    RESULTID NUMBER  NULL,
    REASON VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PREVIOUSVALUE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    VALUE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_RESULT_LOG IS '样品测试任务结果修改记录表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.ORDERTASKID IS '样品测试id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.RESULTID IS '样品测试结果编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.REASON IS '修改结果原因';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.PREVIOUSVALUE IS '修改前结果';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.VALUE IS '修改后结果';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_RESULT_LOG.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD(
    ID NUMBER(32)  NOT NULL,
    EQUIPTID NUMBER(32)  NULL,
    MAINTID NUMBER(32)  NULL,
    VERIFYORG VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CERTNO VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CERTCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CERTEFFECTDATE DATE  NULL,
    STATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    STARTSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    AIRSATURATION VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    ADDICE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    MAINTSITUATION VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    COMPLETEFLAG VARCHAR2(1)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD IS '仪器维护计划';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD.equiptId IS '设备id';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD.maintId IS '维护事件id';

 );

    ID NUMBER(32)  NOT NULL,

    QUANTITY NUMBER(15)  NULL,
    PRICE NUMBER(15,4)  NULL,
    TOTALAMOUNT NUMBER(15,4)  NULL,
    PURCHASETIME DATE  NULL,
    FACTORYNO VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    RESPONSITYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    USEORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    USEMODIFY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    STOREPLACE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_FIXEDASSET IS '固定资产管理表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_FIXEDASSET.assetName IS '资产名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_FIXEDASSET.model IS '型号';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_ENV_RECORD(
     ID VARCHAR(18) NOT NULL,
     MONITORCOMPANY VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL,
     ORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     ORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL,
     ROOM VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     MONITORDATE DATE DEFAULT NULL,
     MONITORTIME VARCHAR(9) DEFAULT NULL,
     TEMPERATURE VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     HUMIDITY VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     PRESSURE VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     RECORDUSERID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     RECORDUSERNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL,
     CREATEDTIME DATE DEFAULT NULL,
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL,
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL,
     CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL
);
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_ENV_RECORD IS '环境记录';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.MONITORCOMPANY IS '监测公司';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.ORGID IS '主部门编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.ORGNAME IS '部门名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.ROOM IS '房间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.MONITORDATE IS '日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.MONITORTIME IS '时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.TEMPERATURE IS '温度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.HUMIDITY IS '湿度';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.PRESSURE IS '气压';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.RECORDUSERID IS '记录人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.RECORDUSERNAME IS '记录人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.createdById IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.createdByName IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.createdTime IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_ENV_RECORD.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_ENV_RECORD ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_ENV_RECORD PRIMARY KEY (ID);

-- 创建菜单语句

VALUES ('100533', '环境记录', '/secure/emc/module/mdm/basemdm/env-records/page/edit-list', '1005', '100533', '环境记录', '001');

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID,AUDITABLE,ATTACHMENT)
VALUES ('T_EMC_ENV_RECORD', '0', '0');

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_BIOLOGY(
    ID number  NOT NULL,
    PARENTID number   NULL,
    SPECIESCODE VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    SPECIESNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    SPECIESENGLISHNAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL

 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_BIOLOGY IS '生物项';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.speciesCode IS '物种编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.speciesName IS '物种名字';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.speciesEngLishName IS '英文名字';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIOLOGY.parentId IS '父id';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_BIOLOGY  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_BIOLOGY PRIMARY KEY (ID);

-- 创建菜单语句
INSERT INTO T_CORE_MENU(ID,MENUNAME,HREF,PARENTID,ORDERNO, MENUINITNAME )
VALUES ('100535', '生物项目分析', '/secure/emc/module/rsm/mdm/biologys/page/edit-list', '1005', '100535', '生物项目分析');

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID,AUDITABLE,ATTACHMENT)
VALUES ('T_EMC_BIOLOGY', '0', '0');

commit;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-30 add by zhangft--------------begin
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM MODIFY (INFOTMPLFIELDID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL);
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM MODIFY (TESTID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL);
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM MODIFY (METHODID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL);
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM MODIFY (ITEMID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
  INFOTMPLID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL,
  MONITORPOINTID NUMBER DEFAULT NULL
);
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT IS '上报模板监测点';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT.INFOTMPLID IS '模板编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT.MONITORPOINTID IS '监测点编码';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_INFO_TMPL_POINT_INFOTMPLID ON T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT (INFOTMPLID);

-- ----------------------------- 2019-12-04 add by qianc--------------begin
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW(
    ID NUMBER  NOT NULL,
    QUALITYCONTROLNO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    REVIEWPURPOSE VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWSCOPE VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWTIME DATE  NULL,
    PARTAKEUSER VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    REVIEWADDRESS VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    REVIEWWAY VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    REVIEWCONTENT VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    PREPAREWORK VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWSTARTTIME DATE  NULL,
    REVIEWENDTIME DATE  NULL,
    REVIEWBASIS VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWDESC VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    ADVISE VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWOPINION VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    YEARTARGET VARCHAR2(540)  NULL,
    REVIEWGROUPUSER VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW IS '管理评审计划';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.ID IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.QUALITYCONTROLNO IS '质控编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWPURPOSE IS '评审目的';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWSCOPE IS '评审范围';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWTIME IS '拟计划评审时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.PARTAKEUSER IS '参与人员';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWADDRESS IS '评审地点';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWWAY IS '评审方式';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWCONTENT IS '评审内容';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.PREPAREWORK IS '准备工作';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWSTARTTIME IS '评审开始时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWENDTIME IS '评审结束时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWBASIS IS '评审依据';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWDESC IS '评审情况描述';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.ADVISE IS '整改措施及具体建议';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWOPINION IS '评审意见';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.YEARTARGET IS '下一年度的目标和活动计划';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.REVIEWGROUPUSER IS '评审组成员';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW.PROCESSSTATUS IS '流程状态';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_QUALITY_CONTROL  (
  ID number NOT NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLCODE varchar2(18) NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLSCOPE varchar2(36) NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLCATEGORY varchar2(36) NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLOBJECT varchar2(540) NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLTARGET varchar2(540) NULL,
  QUALITYCONTROLORGNAME varchar2(270) NULL,
  REQUEST varchar2(540) NULL,
  TIME varchar2(540) NULL,
  REMARK varchar2(540) NULL,
  STANDARDMTL varchar2(270) NULL,
  TESTBASIS varchar2(540) NULL,
  PARTAKEUSER varchar2(270) NULL,
  TECHNICALREQUEST varchar2(540) NULL,
  ENVCONDITION varchar2(270) NULL,
  RESOURCECONFIG varchar2(270) NULL,
  TESTORDER varchar2(270) NULL,
  FEASIBILITY varchar2(540) NULL,
  REVIEWUSER varchar2(270) NULL,
  EVALUATEUSER varchar2(270) NULL,
  EVALUATEDATE date NULL,
  ORIGINALRECORD varchar2(540) NULL,
  CONTROLRESULT varchar2(540) NULL,
  INFLUENCE varchar2(540) NULL,
  POTENTIALFACTOR varchar2(540) NULL,
  PRECAUTION varchar2(540) NULL,
  PROCESSSTATUS varchar2(36) NULL,
  CREATEDBYID varchar2(36) NULL,
  CREATEDBYNAME  varchar2(60) NULL,
  CREATEDTIME date NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGID varchar2(36) NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME varchar2(270) NULL
);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_PRECAUTION (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
  PRECAUTECODE VARCHAR2(18) DEFAULT NULL,
  SOURCE VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  SOURCENO VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  IMPLEMENTORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) DEFAULT NULL,
  IMPLEMENTLEADER VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  PARTAKEUSER VARCHAR2(300) DEFAULT NULL,
  REASION VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  PRECAUTION VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  VERIFYMETHOD VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  COMPLETEDATE DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  VERIFYUSER VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDTIME DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_PRECAUTION IS '预防措施计划';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.PRECAUTECODE IS '质控编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.SOURCE IS '来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.SOURCENO IS '来源编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.IMPLEMENTORGNAME IS '实施部门';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.IMPLEMENTLEADER IS '实施负责人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.PARTAKEUSER IS '参加人员';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.REASION IS '原因';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.PRECAUTION IS '拟采取预防措施';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.VERIFYMETHOD IS '拟采取验证方法';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.COMPLETEDATE IS '预计完成时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.VERIFYUSER IS '验证人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PRECAUTION.PROCESSSTATUS IS '流程状态';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_PRECAUTION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_PRECAUTION PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE "t_emc_internal_audit" (
  "id" NUMBER(32) NOT NULL,
  "qualityControlNo" NVARCHAR2(18),
  "auditPurpose" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "auditBasis" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "auditOrg" NVARCHAR2(270),
  "auditGroupUser" NVARCHAR2(360),
  "auditTime" DATE,
  "auditContent" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "userTrain" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "recordTable" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "startTime" DATE,
  "endTime" DATE,
  "auditDate" DATE,
  "lastAuditDate" DATE,
  "division" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "auditSituation" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "inconformity" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "manageSystem" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "correct" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "auditRequest" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "auditReview" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "sendScope" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "CREATEDBYID" NVARCHAR2(36),
  "CREATEDBYNAME" NVARCHAR2(60),
  "CREATEDTIME" DATE,
  "CREATEDBYORGID" NVARCHAR2(36),
  "CREATEDBYORGNAME" NVARCHAR2(270),
  "PROCESSSTATUS" NVARCHAR2(36)
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."id" IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."qualityControlNo" IS '质控编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditPurpose" IS '审核目的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditBasis" IS '审核依据';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditOrg" IS '被审核部门';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditGroupUser" IS '内审组成员';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditTime" IS '拟计划审核时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditContent" IS '拟计划审核内容';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."userTrain" IS '内审人员培训（必要时）';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."recordTable" IS '记录表格';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."startTime" IS '内审开始时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."endTime" IS '内审结束时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditDate" IS '审核日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."lastAuditDate" IS '上次审核日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."division" IS '审核成员分工及审核部门';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditSituation" IS '审核情况综述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."inconformity" IS '不符合项目归纳分类及分布项';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."manageSystem" IS '受审方的管理体系执行情况的整体综合评价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."correct" IS '纠正措施或预防措施执行情况';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditRequest" IS '审核后续活动要求';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."auditReview" IS '审核综述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."sendScope" IS '发送范围';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."CREATEDBYID" IS '制单人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."CREATEDBYNAME" IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."CREATEDTIME" IS '制单日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."CREATEDBYORGID" IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "t_emc_internal_audit"."CREATEDBYORGNAME" IS '制单人单位名称';

  "id" NUMBER NOT NULL,
  "qualityControlNo" NVARCHAR2(18),
  "source" NVARCHAR2(36),
  "sourceNo" NVARCHAR2(60),
  "factDesc" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "inconformityBasis" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "correctActivity" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "workCategory" NVARCHAR2(36),
  "evaluation" NVARCHAR2(36),
  "inconformityOrg" NVARCHAR2(270),
  "findUser" NVARCHAR2(60),
  "findTime" DATE,
  "orderNo" NVARCHAR2(60),
  "correctAction" NVARCHAR2(540),
  "completeTime" DATE,

  "CREATEDTIME" DATE,

);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CORRECTION (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
  CORRECTIONCODE VARCHAR2(18) DEFAULT NULL,
  SOURCE VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  SOURCENO VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  FACTDESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  BASIS VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  RESPONSIBLEORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) DEFAULT NULL,
  RESPONSIBLELEADER VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  DISCOVERDATE DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  DISCOVERUSER VARCHAR2(300) DEFAULT NULL,
  CORRECTION VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  COMPLETESITUATION VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  VERIFYRESULT VARCHAR2(540) DEFAULT NULL,
  COMPLETEDATE DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  VERIFYUSER VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) DEFAULT NULL,
  CREATEDTIME DATE DEFAULT NULL,
  PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CORRECTION IS '纠正措施计划';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CORRECTIONCODE IS '质控编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.SOURCE IS '来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.SOURCENO IS '来源编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.FACTDESCRIPTION IS '事实描述';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.BASIS IS '依据';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.RESPONSIBLEORGNAME IS '责任部门';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.RESPONSIBLELEADER IS '负责人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.DISCOVERDATE IS '发现时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.DISCOVERUSER IS '发现人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CORRECTION IS '纠正措施';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.COMPLETESITUATION IS '完成情况';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.VERIFYRESULT IS '验证结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.COMPLETEDATE IS '预计完成时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.VERIFYUSER IS '验证人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CORRECTION.PROCESSSTATUS IS '流程状态';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_CORRECTION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_CORRECTION PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_SOL(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    SOLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    SOLSOURCE VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    SOLNO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    SOLCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CALUCATE VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    PARENTSOLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TAKEWEIGHTORVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    PARTAKEUSERID VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    PARTAKEUSERNAME VARCHAR2(600)  NULL,
    PREPAREUSERID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TABCATEGORY VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CALIBRATEDATE DATE NULL,
    CORRECTFACTOR NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    REAGENTNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    REAGENTLEVEL VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REAGENTMANUFACTOR VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    BALANCEMODEL VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    DRYTEMPERATURE VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    REAGENTWEIGHT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    SOLVENTNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CONSTANTVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    PREPARETEMPERATURE VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    PREPARECONCENT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    CALIBRATESOLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CALIBRATESOLCONCENT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    TOCALIBRATESOLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    TOCALIBRATESOLVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    BURETTEVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    CALIBRATEDCONCENT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    ORIGINALCONCENT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    TRANSFERVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    DILUTIONNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PREPARECONSTANTVOLUME NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    PREPAREDCONCENT NUMBER(15,6)  NULL,
    PREPAREDATE DATE NULL,
    SOLPREPAREDATE DATE NULL,
    VOLUMEUNITNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    CONCENTUNITNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    WEIGHTUNITNAME VARCHAR2(64)  NULL,
    VALIDDATE DATE NULL,
    SCRIPT VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_SOL IS '标准溶液配制';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.solName IS '标准溶液名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.solSource IS '标准溶液来源';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.solNo IS '标准溶液编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.solCategory IS '溶液类别';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.calucate IS '是否参与计算';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.parentSolName IS '上级标准物质/溶液';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.takeWeightOrVolume IS '取用重量/体积';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.partakeUserId IS '共同完成人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.partakeUserName IS '共同完成人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.prepareUserId IS '配制人';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.tabCategory IS '页签类型';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.calibrateDate IS '标定日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.correctFactor IS '校正系数K';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.reagentLevel IS '试剂等级';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SOL.reagentManufactor IS '试剂生产厂家及编号';

 );

    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    PROJID NUMBER NULL,
    PROJNO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    SAMPLEID NUMBER NULL,
    SAMPLENO VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    EQUIPTID NUMBER NULL,
    USEDTIME DATE NULL,
    BEFOREUSESTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    AFTERUSESTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    USERID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    USERNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_USE_RECORD IS '仪器使用记录';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_USE_RECORD.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_USE_RECORD.projId IS '项目编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_USE_RECORD.projNo IS '项目编号';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_EQUIPT_USE_RECORD.sampleNo IS '采样单编号';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    RELATECODE VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    RELATENAME VARCHAR2(120)  NULL,
    RELATESCRIPT VARCHAR2(1080) NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION IS '数据相关性表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.relateCode IS '关联代码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.relateName IS '关联名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.relateScript IS '关联公式';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    CORRELATIONID NUMBER NULL,
    TESTID NUMBER NULL,
    METHODID NUMBER NULL,
    ITEMID NUMBER NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM IS '数据相关性分析项';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.id IS '主键';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.correlationId IS '相关性编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.testId IS '测试代码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.methodId IS '方法编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.itemId IS '分析项目';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.createdById IS '制单人编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.createdByName IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.createdTime IS '制单时间';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.createdByOrgId IS '制单人单位编码';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM.createdByOrgName IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_DCI_CORRELATIONID ON T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM (CORRELATIONID);

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INDEX_SET ADD (INDEXHIGHLIMIT1 varchar(30));
UPDATE T_EMC_INDEX_SET SET INDEXLOWLIMIT1=INDEXLOWLIMIT, INDEXHIGHLIMIT1=INDEXHIGHLIMIT;
UPDATE T_EMC_INDEX_SET SET INDEXLOWLIMIT=NULL, INDEXHIGHLIMIT=NULL;
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INDEX_SET MODIFY (INDEXLOWLIMIT varchar(30));
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INDEX_SET MODIFY (INDEXHIGHLIMIT varchar(30));
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_SET.INDEXLOWLIMIT IS '指标低限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_SET.INDEXHIGHLIMIT IS '指标高限';
UPDATE T_EMC_INDEX_SET SET INDEXLOWLIMIT=INDEXLOWLIMIT1, INDEXHIGHLIMIT=INDEXHIGHLIMIT1;
COMMIT;

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW (
  ID number NOT NULL,
  STDNAME varchar2(600),
  EXPIREDATE date,
  INDEX1 varchar2(300),
  INDEX2 varchar2(300),
  INDEXITEMID number,
  INDEXLOWLIMIT varchar2(30),
  INDEXHIGHLIMIT varchar2(30),
  TEXTINDEXVALUE varchar2(1500),
  UNIT varchar2(240),
  CREATEDBYID varchar2(36),
  CREATEDBYNAME varchar2(60),
  CREATEDTIME date,
  CREATEDBYORGID varchar2(36),
  CREATEDBYORGNAME varchar2(270)
);

COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW IS '指标预览表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.STDNAME IS '标准名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.EXPIREDATE IS '过期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.INDEX1 IS '指标1';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.INDEX2 IS '指标2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.INDEXITEMID IS '指标项目';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.INDEXLOWLIMIT IS '指标低限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.INDEXHIGHLIMIT IS '指标高限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.TEXTINDEXVALUE IS '文本指标值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.UNIT IS '单位';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.CREATEDTIME IS '制单日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INDEX_PREVIEW.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_WATER_RESULT  (
  ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
  INFOCONFIGID NUMBER NULL,
  MONITORDATE DATE NULL,
  MONITORPOINTNAME VARCHAR2(210) NULL,
  YEAR VARCHAR2(12) NULL,
  MONTH VARCHAR2(12) NULL,
  DAY VARCHAR2(12) NULL,
  AVERAGERESULT VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
  WATERQUALITYCATEGORY VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
  PULLYTEINDEX VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
  EXCEEDSTANDARD VARCHAR2(30) NULL,
  OVERMULTIME VARCHAR2(18) NULL,
  EUTROPHICATION VARCHAR2(18) NULL,
  ITEMALIASNAME VARCHAR2(128) NULL,
  TYPE VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60) NULL,
  CREATEDTIME DATE  NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270) NULL
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."ID" IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."INFOCONFIGID" IS '综合配置编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."MONITORDATE" IS '监测日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."MONITORPOINTNAME" IS '监测点名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."YEAR" IS '年';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."MONTH" IS '月份';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."DAY" IS '日';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."AVERAGERESULT" IS '均值结果';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."WATERQUALITYCATEGORY" IS '水质类别';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."PULLYTEINDEX" IS '污染指数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."EXCEEDSTANDARD" IS '是否超标';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."OVERMULTIME" IS '超标倍数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."EUTROPHICATION" IS '富营养指数';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."ITEMALIASNAME" IS '分析项别名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."TYPE" IS '均值类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."CREATEDBYID" IS '制单人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."CREATEDBYNAME" IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."CREATEDTIME" IS '制单日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."CREATEDBYORGID" IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT"."CREATEDBYORGNAME" IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON TABLE "T_EMC_WATER_RESULT" IS '地表水计算表';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_WATER_RESULT
  ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_WATER_RESULT primary key (ID);

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_PROJECT ADD (SAMPLE VARCHAR2(3));
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PROJECT.SAMPLE IS '是否采样';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY ADD (SHOWSAMPLE VARCHAR2(3));
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY.SHOWSAMPLE IS '显示是否采样';
UPDATE T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY set SHOWSAMPLE = '0'  where SHOWSAMPLE IS NULL;
UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT A SET A.SAMPLE = (SELECT	B.SAMPLE FROM	T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY B WHERE	A.BIZCATEGORYID = B.ID);
COMMIT;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ENDDATE DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR2(1)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    ACTIVATEDTIME DATE NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT IS '附件信息表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ENDDATE IS '到期日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ACTIVATEDFLAG IS '启用标志';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ACTIVATEDBYID IS '启用人ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT.ACTIVATEDTIME IS '启用时间';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_HOME_ATTACHMENT PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ATTACHMENTID NUMBER NULL,
    USERID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION IS '附件权限表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION.ATTACHMENTID IS '附件信息id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION.USERID IS '可见人编码';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERM_ATTAID ON T_EMC_HOME_ATTA_PERMISSION (ATTACHMENTID);

CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA" (
"ID" NUMBER(20,0) NOT NULL,
"TABLENAME" VARCHAR2(66) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDONE" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDTWO" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDTHREE" NUMBER(20,0) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDONENAME" VARCHAR2(66) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDTWONAME" VARCHAR2(66) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"FIELDTHREENAME" VARCHAR2(66) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"CREATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"CREATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(60) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"CREATEDTIME" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"CREATEDBYORGID" VARCHAR2(36) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"CREATEDBYORGNAME" VARCHAR2(255) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"ACTIVATEDFLAG" VARCHAR2(1) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"ACTIVATEDBYID" VARCHAR2(32) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"ACTIVATEDBYNAME" VARCHAR2(64) DEFAULT NULL NULL,
"ACTIVATEDTIME" DATE DEFAULT NULL NULL,
PRIMARY KEY ("ID")
)
NOCOMPRESS
NOPARALLEL ;
COMMENT ON TABLE "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA" IS '生物查表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."ID" IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."TABLENAME" IS '记录表名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDONE" IS '名称1数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDTWO" IS '名称2数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDTHREE" IS '名称3数量';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDONENAME" IS '名称1';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDTWONAME" IS '名称2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."FIELDTHREENAME" IS '名称3';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."CREATEDBYID" IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."CREATEDBYNAME" IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."CREATEDTIME" IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."CREATEDBYORGID" IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."CREATEDBYORGNAME" IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."ACTIVATEDFLAG" IS '启用标志';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."ACTIVATEDBYNAME" IS '启用人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA"."ACTIVATEDTIME" IS '启用时间';

)

NOPARALLEL ;

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."FIELDTWO" IS '字段2';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."FIELDTHREE" IS '字段3';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."CONTENT" IS '内容';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."CREATEDBYID" IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."CREATEDBYNAME" IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_FETCH_DATA_LINE"."CREATEDBYORGNAME" IS '制单人单位名称';

    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    TMPLNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,

    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60),
    CREATEDTIME DATE,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36),
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270),
    ACTIVATEDFLAG    VARCHAR2(1),
    ACTIVATEDBYID    VARCHAR2(36),
    ACTIVATEDBYNAME  VARCHAR2(60),
    ACTIVATEDTIME    DATE
 );

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_TMPL.CREATEDTIME IS '制单日期';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_REPORT_TMPL.ACTIVATEDBYNAME IS '启用人名称';

  ID               NUMBER NOT NULL,
  BIZCATEGORYID    NUMBER ,

  CREATEDBYID      VARCHAR2(36),
  CREATEDBYNAME    VARCHAR2(72),
  CREATEDTIME      DATE,
  CREATEDBYORGID   VARCHAR2(36),
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(72),
  ACTIVATEDFLAG    VARCHAR2(3),
  ACTIVATEDBYID    VARCHAR2(36),
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME  VARCHAR2(72),
  ACTIVATEDTIME    DATE
);

  IS '业务类型常用项目表';

  IS '主键';

  IS '业务类型ID';

  IS '项目名称';

  IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT.CREATEDBYNAME
  IS '制单人名称';

  IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT.CREATEDBYORGID
  IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT.CREATEDBYORGNAME
  IS '制单人单位名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT.ACTIVATEDFLAG
  IS '是否启用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT.ACTIVATEDBYID
  IS '启用人编码';

  IS '启用人名称';

  IS '启用时间';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_CONTRACT(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    ENTRUSTORG VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    CONTRACTNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    CONTRACTNO VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    TOTALPRICE VARCHAR2(36) NULL,
    CONTRACESOURCE VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    MONITORPURPOSE VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    MONITORORDER VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    CMAF VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    CONTRACTREPORT VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    OTHERINFO VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    REVIEWER VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    REVIEWDATE DATE NULL,
    REVIEWADDRESS VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    BEGINDATE DATE NULL,
    ENDDATE DATE NULL,
    REVIEWOPINION VARCHAR2(600)  NULL,
    YEAR VARCHAR2(18)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_CONTRACT IS '合同表';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.ENTRUSTORG IS '委托单位';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.CONTRACTNAME IS '合同名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.CONTRACTNO IS '合同编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.TOTALPRICE IS '总价';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.CONTRACESOURCE IS '合同来源';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.MONITORPURPOSE IS '监测目的';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.MONITORORDER IS '监测样品';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.CMAF IS '资质认定';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.CONTRACTREPORT IS '报告';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.OTHERINFO IS '其他';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.REVIEWER IS '评审人员';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.REVIEWDATE IS '评审日期';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_CONTRACT.REVIEWADDRESS IS '评审地点';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_FILE_MODIFY(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    FILEID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    FILENAME VARCHAR2(66)  NULL,
    MODIFYINGCONTENT VARCHAR2(1200)  NULL,
    MODIFIEDCONTENT VARCHAR2(1200)  NULL,
    MODIFYREASON VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    IMPLEMENTDATE DATE NULL,
    AUDITORID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    AUDITORNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CONFIRMERID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CONFIRMERNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_FILE_MODIFY IS '文件修改';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_FILE_MODIFY.ID IS '主键';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_PURCH(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    PURCHASECATEGORY VARCHAR2(66)  NULL,
    INSTRUCTION VARCHAR2(1200)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(360)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PURCH.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PURCH.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PURCH.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_PURCH.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_PURCH ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_PURCH PRIMARY KEY (ID);

    ID NUMBER(36)  NOT NULL,

    REMARK VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,

    CONFIRMERNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,

    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_QUOTATION_REGISTER(
    ID NUMBER(20)  NOT NULL,
    STATUS VARCHAR2(66)  NULL,
    QUOTATIONNO VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    QUOTATIONNAME VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    COREPERSONCODE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    COREPERSONNAME VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CUSTOMERCODE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CUSTOMERNAME VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CUSTOMERADDRESS VARCHAR2(150)  NULL,
    CUSTOMERPHONE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    CONTACTSCODE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CONTACTSNAME VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CONTACTSPHONE VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CONTACTSFAX VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    COMPANYNAME VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    COMPANYADDRESS VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    COMPANYFAX VARCHAR2(33)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_QUOTATION_REGISTER IS '报价单登记表';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QUOTATION_REGISTER.STATUS IS '状态';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_QUOTATION_REGISTER.CONTACTSNAME IS '联系人名称';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_INVOICE(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    SYSNO VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    INVOICENAME VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    INVOICENO VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    INVOICECATEGORY VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    MAKEDATE DATE NULL,
    DRAWERID VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    DRAWER VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    INVOICEAMOUNT VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    CONTRACTID VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    CONTRACTNAME VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    PROJECTID VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    PROJECTNAME VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    DONEFLAG VARCHAR2(3)  NULL,
    REMARK VARCHAR2(300)  NULL,
    AUDITUSERID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    AUDITUSER VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_INVOICE IS '开票记录';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.SYSNO IS '系统流水号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.INVOICENAME IS '发票名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.INVOICENO IS '发票编号';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.INVOICECATEGORY IS '发票类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.MAKEDATE IS '开票时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.DRAWERID IS '开票人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.DRAWER IS '开票人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.INVOICEAMOUNT IS '开票金额';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CONTRACTID IS '关联合同id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CONTRACTNAME IS '关联合同';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.PROJECTID IS '关联项目id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.PROJECTNAME IS '关联项目';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.DONEFLAG IS '是否回款完成';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.REMARK IS '备注';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.AUDITUSERID IS '审核人id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.AUDITUSER IS '审核人';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.PROCESSSTATUS IS '流程状态';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';

COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_INVOICE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

CREATE TABLE "T_EMC_QC_ITEM" (
  "ID" NUMBER(20) NOT NULL,
  "TESTMETHODID" NUMBER(20),
  "ITEMNAME" VARCHAR2(180 BYTE),
  "ITEMALIASNAME" VARCHAR2(180 BYTE),
  "LOWLIMIT" VARCHAR2(36 BYTE),
  "HIGHLIMIT" VARCHAR2(36 BYTE),
  "REVISIONRULE" VARCHAR2(36 BYTE),
  "QCCATEGORYID" VARCHAR2(400 BYTE),
  "QCCATEGORYNAME" VARCHAR2(600 BYTE) 
);

COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."ID" IS 'ID主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."TESTMETHODID" IS '测试方法ID';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."ITEMNAME" IS '分析项名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."ITEMALIASNAME" IS '分析项别名';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."LOWLIMIT" IS '检出低限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."HIGHLIMIT" IS '检出高限';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."REVISIONRULE" IS '修约规则';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."QCCATEGORYID" IS 'QC中登录';
COMMENT ON COLUMN "T_EMC_QC_ITEM"."QCCATEGORYNAME" IS 'QC类型名称(多个)';

ALTER TABLE "T_EMC_QC_ITEM" ADD CONSTRAINT "PK_T_EMC_QC_ITEM" PRIMARY KEY ("ID");

COMMIT;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    NAME VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    FIELD VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    BUSINESSCATEGORY VARCHAR2(90)  NULL,
    LABORATORY VARCHAR2(150)  NULL,
    ACTIVEDFLAG VARCHAR2(3)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE IS '盖章打印规则';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.NAME IS '规则名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.FIELD IS '领域';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.BUSINESSCATEGORY IS '业务类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.LABORATORY IS '实验室';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.ACTIVEDFLAG IS '是否启用';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_SEAL_RULE PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS(
    ID NUMBER NOT NULL,
    SEALRULEID NUMBER NULL,
    SEALGROUPNO VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    SEALGROUPNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    SEALNO VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    SEALNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    PAGETYPE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    FIXEDPAGENUM VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    CUSTOMPAGENUM VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    XVALUE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    YVALUE VARCHAR2(30)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR2(60)  NULL,
    CREATEDTIME DATE NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR2(36)  NULL,
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR2(270)  NULL
 );
COMMENT ON TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS IS '盖章打印规则详情';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.ID IS '主键';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.SEALRULEID IS '盖章打印规则id';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.SEALGROUPNO IS '图章组编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.SEALGROUPNAME IS '图章组名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.SEALNO IS '图章编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.SEALNAME IS '图章名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.PAGETYPE IS '页码类型';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.FIXEDPAGENUM IS '固定页码值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CUSTOMPAGENUM IS '自定义页码值';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.XVALUE IS 'X坐标';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.YVALUE IS 'Y坐标';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CREATEDBYID IS '制单人编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CREATEDBYNAME IS '制单人名称';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CREATEDTIME IS '制单时间';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CREATEDBYORGID IS '制单人单位编码';
COMMENT ON COLUMN T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS.CREATEDBYORGNAME IS '制单人单位名称';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS_SEALRULEID ON T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS (SEALRULEID);
